## Biquaternion Universe Relativity

### The Biquaternion Universe and Its Objects

### The Lorentz Transformation on the Informational Sector

#### Observables, states and forms

## Biquaternion Universe Symmetry Breaking

### From the SU(4) Model to the Standard Model

<!-- one transition at M_C and one audit: the SU(4) group breaks into the four-factor group, which
     has no width, and the Standard Model is what remains below that scale once the heavy fields are
     removed; it is then counted against its own boundary. The toolbox the block opens with is
     *Action and Force in the Biquaternion Universe*, the opening subsection of *Generalities* above. -->

#### The Transition: One Vacuum, Four Factors, One Scale

the obliqueness of the frame, Gram $2$ on the diagonal and $1$ off it, and its orthogonal sibling $A_j=W_{jj}=\sum_kM_k-2M_j$ with Gram $4\,\mathbb1$, the integer matrix $J-2\mathbb1$ between the two and the trace form diagonal only on the second; **why the construction needs a line outside the permutation**, the $-1$ on $\epsilon_t$ being what makes the triple traceless, so that in $\mathfrak{su}(3)$ the democratic triple $(1,1,-2)$ and its permutations sum to zero and have rank two, and the orthogonality of a size-three orbit reducing to $(v_t-3\beta)(v_t+\beta)=0$, whose two families are $A$ and $(3,-5,1,1)$; the dimerous-electron reading of charge and interference with the fusion condition $\Delta\alpha=2\pi N$ and the de Broglie relation it is meant to recover, with the factor of two in the printed chain unpinned; and, at the end of that section, the finite Newtonian realisation of the same fusion picture in the authors' separate implicit-worldline sub-programme (arXiv:1402.6158) — the worldline as the root set of $F_A(x_1,x_2,s)=0$, the copies as its roots, real roots as R-particles and complex conjugate pairs as C-particles of double effective mass, the events as multiple roots with the vanishing-Jacobian condition, the nine-root worked example with its six event instants recomputed here, and the Vieta rather than Noether origin of its conservation laws, which fails over a noncommutative algebra, the paper carrying no hypercomplex algebra and relativising on the Euclidean interval; the random complex time, the chronon and the kinematic-statistical irreversibility from the positivity of $\delta T$; the 2016 note's **local algebra** — Grgin's covariant multiplication law identified with the biquaternion multiplication (one sign giving the ordinary product and the other the reversed one, verified), the local $L$-algebra of the tetrad $\Sigma_\mu=h^\alpha_{\ \mu}\sigma_\alpha$ with the induced metric $g_{\mu\nu}=h^\alpha_{\ \mu}h^\beta_{\ \nu}\eta_{\alpha\beta}$ and the required **unit U-field** $E_\mu$ (distinct from the unitary field $U=FF^\dagger$), the **effective metric** $\tilde g_{\rho\lambda}=2E_\rho E_\lambda-g_{\rho\lambda}$ that is Euclidean in the flat case, the coincidence $g^*_{\mu\nu}=\tfrac14C^\beta_{\ \mu\alpha}C^\alpha_{\ \nu\beta}=\tilde g_{\mu\nu}$ between the metric the connection is built from and the metric computed from the structure functions alone (exact in the rest frame of the unit field, and false once the unit field is tilted, the discrepancy being quadratic in its spatial part), and the induced Weyl–Cartan connection $\Gamma=\gamma+G$ with torsion and non-metricity $\nabla_\rho g_{\mu\nu}=-2g_{\mu\nu}\tilde A_{\Phi,\rho}$, $\tilde A_{\Phi,\rho}=(2E_\rho E_\lambda-g_{\rho\lambda})A^\lambda$, so that the Weyl vector is the potential read through the effective metric; and the note's own statement that the conditions determining the metric and the U-field are left to future work; the source's survey of the source problem of Kerr-type solutions — the two-valued field, the disk and oblate-spheroid cuts of the Kerr ring, and the conclusion that no source should be sought; what the programme claims and what it does not; the 2023 sequel's implicit worldline and its twistor proof that every such worldline induces the null shear-free congruence, and with it the light cone, together with the kinematical $H=1/t$ of the receding roots, refused as a cosmological result; an external programme, recorded and not endorsed. -->
